However, we find that OPSD consistently fails on long chain-of-thought (long-CoT) reasoning models, yielding at best marginal gains while destabilizing the reflective reasoning capability these models depend on. Through a novel decomposition of the teacher's supervision signal, we identify the root cause: the teacher's supervision is dominated by a reference-induced component that drives rote memorization of reference-specific shortcuts, while the question-conditioned, inference-transferable component is ignored or actively opposed. Based on this diagnosis, we propose a two-step solution. First, we construct a reference-only teacher (the same model conditioned on the reference without the question) to isolate the non-transferable component of the supervision signal; the residual after subtracting this component captures the question-conditioned, inference-transferable correction. Second, we use pointwise mutual information (PMI) as the mechanism to transform this residual into a well-formed PMI target distribution that the student can directly distill from, filtering out the reference-induced shortcut. Experiments on four long-CoT models across two datasets demonstrate consistent improvements over both the base model and standard OPSD, while preserving the models' natural epistemic behavior throughout training.

</details>


### [185] [Copewell: A Multi-Agent Swarm Architecture for Equitable Mental Wellness Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02245)
*Seren Yenikent,Jack Vinijtrongjit,Katherine Ng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Copewell，一个基于多智能体群系统的人工智能心理健康支持平台，通过多源评估框架、效价-唤醒情绪映射和双模式干预三大技术创新，结合隐私优先架构和伦理监督机制，旨在扩大心理健康服务的可及性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球近10亿人受心理健康障碍影响，但中低收入国家75%的患者因劳动力短缺、成本障碍和污名化而无法获得治疗。现有AI健康方案主要依赖单一模式对话界面，存在高放弃率且无法根据用户动态情绪状态提供可测量的即时缓解。

Method: (1) 多源评估框架：整合自我报告、生理和情境数据以缓解算法偏差；(2) 基于Russell情感环状模型的效价-唤醒情绪映射，将用户路由至专门的AI智能体；(3) 双模式干预：结合对话支持与基于证据的感觉健康方案。

Result: 早期从业者参与和beta部署为设计决策提供了依据，并确定了未来实证评估的方向。

Conclusion: 通过展示技术架构如何从初始阶段即可实现公平与安全原则，为负责任AI discourse做出贡献。

Abstract: Mental health disorders affect nearly one billion people globally, yet 75% of individuals in low- and middle-income countries receive no treatment due to workforce shortages, cost barriers, and stigma. Current AI-powered wellness solutions predominantly rely on single-mode conversational interfaces that suffer high abandonment rates and fail to provide measurable, immediate relief calibrated to users' dynamic emotional states. This paper presents Copewell, a novel multi-agent swarm system designed to expand access to mental wellness support through human-centered AI principles. Our architecture introduces three technical innovations: (1) a multi-source assessment framework integrating self-reported, physiological, and contextual data to mitigate algorithmic bias; (2) valence-arousal emotion mapping using Russell's Circumplex Model of Affect to route users to specialized AI agents; and (3) dual-mode intervention delivery combining conversational support with evidence-based sensory wellness protocols. We examine the sociotechnical design considerations underlying Copewell's development, including a privacy-first architecture, embedded ethical oversight through a dedicated Ethics Supervisor agent, and participatory design informed by mental health practitioners. Early practitioner engagement and beta deployment inform design decisions and identify directions for future empirical evaluation. This work contributes to responsible AI discourse by demonstrating how technical architecture can operationalize equity and safety principles from inception.

</details>


### [186] [AgenticSTS: A Bounded-Memory Testbed for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02255)
*Xiangchen Cheng,Yunwei Jiang,Jianwen Sun,Zizhen Li,Chuanhao Li,Xiangcheng Cao,Yihao Liu,Fanrui Zhang,Li Jin,Kaipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种有界记忆契约（Bounded Memory Contract），通过类型化检索（typed retrieval）替代传统拼接式上下文，使LLM智能体在长周期决策中保持提示长度有界，并支持单层消融分析。在《杀戮尖塔2》游戏中，基于该契约的智能体在启用策略技能层时胜率从3/10提升至6/10（n=10，Fisher精确检验p≈0.37，方向性而非统计显著）。论文发布了包含298条完整轨迹的可复现测试平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统长周期LLM智能体通过拼接历史观测、工具调用和反思来构建上下文，导致提示变得冗长混杂，难以隔离单一记忆组件的影响。作者旨在提出一种替代的记忆契约，使提示长度有界且支持独立消融分析，以系统研究显式记忆层如何塑造长周期决策。

Method: 提出有界记忆契约：每次决策由类型化检索组装全新的用户消息，不拼接原始跨决策转录。实例化于《杀戮尖塔2》游戏，通过固定A0消融实验对比无存储基线（3/10胜率）与启用策略技能层（6/10胜率）的表现，并进行跨骨干网络探测和公共累积上下文基线比较。

Result: 启用策略技能层时胜率从3/10提升至6/10（方向性差异，Fisher精确检验p≈0.37，未达统计显著性）。公开基准中前沿LLM在最低难度下零胜率，开发者报告人类胜率为16%。发布了包含298条完整轨迹、冻结记忆/技能快照、提示记录和分析脚本的可复现测试平台。

Conclusion: 有界记忆契约提供了一种可复现的方法论来研究显式记忆层对长周期LLM智能体决策的影响，尽管当前实验结果仅为方向性而非统计决定性。该测试平台为后续研究提供了标准化的验证工具。

Abstract: Memory for a long-horizon LLM agent is a contract about what each future decision is allowed to see. The simplest contract appends past observations, tool calls, and reflections to every prompt, which makes prior context easy to access but also turns it into a jumbled mixture in which the effect of any single memory component is hard to isolate. We introduce and instrument an alternative bounded contract: every decision is made from a fresh user message assembled by typed retrieval, with no raw cross-decision transcript appended. The prompt thus stays bounded across runs of any length, and any single layer can be ablated in isolation. We instantiate the contract in Slay the Spire 2, a closed-rule stochastic deck-building game whose runs require hundreds of tactical and strategic decisions. A public online benchmark of frontier LLMs on the same game reports zero wins at the lowest difficulty across five configurations, and the developer-reported human win rate at the same difficulty is 16%; the task is hard but not saturated. Within our harness, a fixed-A0 ablation shows the largest observed difference when triggered strategic skills are enabled: the no-store baseline wins 3/10 games and adding the skill layer 6/10. At this sample size the comparison is directional rather than statistically decisive (Fisher exact p\approx0.37); a cross-backbone probe and public accumulating-context baselines are reported as operational comparisons rather than controlled tests of the contract variable itself. We release a reproducible testbed: 298 completed trajectories with condition tags, frozen memory/skill snapshots, prompt records, and analysis scripts -- an agent design and a validated, reusable methodology for studying how explicit memory layers shape long-horizon LLM-agent decisions.

</details>


### [187] [A Hippocampus for Linear Attention: An Exact Memory for What the Recurrent State Forgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02303)
*Wanyun Cui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HOLA (Hippocampal Linear Attention) 为线性注意力模型添加了一个有界精确KV缓存作为海马体补全，形成半参数化测试时记忆系统，在不使用学习型驱逐模块的情况下，通过残差驱动的缓存写入和去耦RMSNorm-gamma读取，实现了在困惑度和长上下文检索上的显著提升，且外推至32k tokens。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力和状态空间模型将前缀压缩为固定大小的循环状态，虽然实现了O(1)内存但存在有损精确记忆的问题：当大量键-值关联竞争时，早期事实会被覆盖，导致"针在海草堆"检索性能下降。

Method: 受互补学习系统启发，HOLA保留delta-rule状态作为压缩记忆，并添加一个有界精确KV缓存作为海马体补全，形成半参数化测试时记忆。缓存写入不使用学习型驱逐模块，而是保留具有较大beta*||e||（实际提交到状态的预测残差）的token；读取时采用去耦的RMSNorm-gamma实现锐利检索而非软平均。

Result: 340M参数在15B SlimPajama tokens上训练，HOLA将Wikitext困惑度从27.32降至22.92（-16.1%），低于全注意力Transformer++（26.88）；LAMBADA困惑度从30.95降至30.26；在线性上下文检索上达到最佳，且在RULER针在海草堆测试中保持最鲁棒性，可外推至32k tokens（训练长度的16倍）。

Conclusion: 通过给线性注意力配备海马体补全，HOLA在保持线性注意力高效记忆压缩能力的同时，有效缓解了有损精确记忆问题，在困惑度、上下文检索和长序列外推方面均展现出显著优势。

Abstract: Linear-attention and state-space language models compress the prefix into a fixed-size recurrent state, yielding O(1) memory at the cost of a lossy exact memory: when many key--value associations compete, earlier facts are overwritten and needle recall degrades. Inspired by Complementary Learning Systems, we give linear attention a hippocampal complement. HOLA (Hippocampal Linear Attention) keeps the usual delta-rule state as a compressive memory and adds a bounded exact KV cache, forming a semiparametric test-time memory: the state models linearly compressible structure, while the cache stores associations that should not be forced through that state. The cache writes without a learned eviction module, keeping tokens with large beta * ||e||, the prediction residual actually committed to the state; a decoupled RMSNorm-gamma cache read then turns these exact KV pairs into sharp retrieval rather than soft averaging. At 340M parameters trained on 15B SlimPajama tokens, HOLA lowers Wikitext perplexity from 27.32 to 22.92 (-16.1%), below a full-attention Transformer++ (26.88), and improves LAMBADA perplexity from 30.95 to 30.26. It also achieves the best linear in-context retrieval and remains much more robust than GDN or a matched HOLA+recency cache on RULER needle-in-a-haystack recall out to 32k tokens (16x its training length).

</details>


### [188] [Grounded autonomous research: a fault-tolerant LLM pipeline from corpus to manuscript in frontier computational physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02329)
*Haonan Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端自主研究管线，能够从11,083篇凝聚态物理arXiv论文出发，自动完成研究构思、方法校准、第一性原理计算和论文撰写，最终生成包含三项实质性物理发现的出版级手稿。通过分布式冗余架构和对抗性审查实现容错，并识别出结构化数值校对作为关键机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿物理科学与机器学习沙盒环境存在本质差异：物理推理主导方法选择、工具链文档不完善、校准必须依赖外部文献锚点。现有自主研究智能体缺乏对文献的实质性核对，容易产生看似合理但不可验证的幻觉结果，亟需一种能在高保真物理科学领域可靠运行的自主研究范式。

Method: 构建端到端管线，包含六个阶段47个独立上下文会话，仅通过磁盘状态共享：1）语料映射与研究构思；2）通过复现已发表参考文献进行方法校准；3）执行第一性原理计算；4）论文撰写。采用三层冗余机制：独立上下文隔离、分布式文献锚定、对抗性审查。设置预架构基线和无导引消融实验以隔离关键机制。

Result: 在交替磁体压电性领域自动生成了三项实质性物理发现和出版级手稿。共发生2,162次文献查阅事件。两个配对失败模式（预架构基线和无导引消融）验证了在校准检查点进行结构化数值对抗是有效的基础锚定机制。故障通过冗余设计得到容错处理：单人审查遗漏的问题被其他会话捕获。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了自主研究智能体在计算物理等高风险科学领域的可行性，其原语化方法、特征化失败模式和量化干预模式为超越计算物理的高保真科学领域的自主研究奠定了基础。关键启示在于：通过结构化数值对抗（而非单纯引用）实现文献锚定是自主科学研究的核心机制。

Abstract: Autonomous-research agents have demonstrated end-to-end LLM automation in machine-learning sandboxes where execution provides calibration. Frontier physical science differs categorically: physical reasoning underlies every methodology choice, toolchains are often underdocumented, and calibration must come from external literature anchors - which unscaffolded agents cite but do not confront, hallucinating plausible, unverifiable results from internal priors. We present a pipeline that runs end-to-end from a corpus of 11,083 recent condensed-matter physics arXiv papers to a publication-grade manuscript with three substantive physics findings (here on altermagnetic piezomagnetism): the agent autonomously conceives a research direction by mapping the corpus, calibrates methodology by reproducing published references, conducts novel first-principles computations, and writes the manuscript - grounded in literature throughout, across 47 fresh-context sessions in six phases sharing only on-disk state, with 2,162 literature-consultation events. Fault tolerance emerges from redundancy: fresh-context isolation, distributed grounding, and adversarial review catch what any single session misses; pre- and post-pilot stages are fully autonomous, and pilot requires bounded human intervention only at reproduction failures - operational knowledge curation, not scientific direction. Two paired failure modes - a pre-architecture baseline and a no-pilot ablation - isolate structurally enforced numerical confrontation at calibration checkpoints as the operative grounding mechanism. The primitives, characterized failure modes, and quantified intervention pattern lay a foundation for autonomous research in high-stakes scientific domains beyond computational physics.

</details>


### [189] [DRIFTLENS: Measuring Memory-Induced Reasoning Drift in Personalized Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02374)
*Xi Fang,Weijie Xu,Yingqiang Ge,Yuhui Xu,Stephanie Eckman,Chandan K. Reddy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出DRIFTLENS框架，用于量化LLM中用户属性记忆对推理轨迹的漂移影响。实验表明记忆注入会引起中等至大的推理漂移，即使最终答案仍然流畅合理。GRPO和DPO后训练方法可部分缓解但无法完全消除此问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的个性化LLM研究主要关注模型输出的内容变化，但忽视了记忆注入对推理过程本身的影响。作者希望探究用户属性记忆是否会改变模型的推理轨迹，而不仅仅是最终答案，并开发一种无需真实标签的量化方法来衡量这种影响。

Method: 提出了DRIFTLENS框架，该框架将每个推理步骤映射到价值类别，通过比较无记忆条件下的推理轨迹与注入用户属性记忆后的推理轨迹之间的差异来量化漂移。在4种LLM和10类用户属性（年龄、职业、残疾等）上进行了实验，并使用GRPO和DPO进行后训练缓解漂移。

Result: 用户属性记忆在所有模型和属性类别上均引起中等至大的推理漂移，显著高于模型的语用噪声基线水平。GRPO和DPO均能降低漂移，但效果并非一致最优，对下游能力、帮助性和指令遵循的影响依赖于具体模型和奖励设置。

Conclusion: 记忆诱导的推理漂移是LLM个性化过程中可测量且仅能被部分缓解的失效模式，需要进一步研究更有效的校准方法。

Abstract: Personalization changes what a model says to a user; we show that it can also change the reasoning trajectory used to justify the response. Modern LLMs personalize interactions by storing user attributes, preferences, and prior context, then injecting this information into future prompts. We study whether such memory reshapes reasoning on open-ended questions where no single ground-truth answer exists. To quantify this effect, we introduce DRIFTLENS, a ground-truth-free framework that maps each expressed reasoning step to a value category and measures divergence between a question's no-memory trajectory and its trajectory under injected user-attribute memory. We first validate that DRIFTLENS distinguishes content-free pragmatic noise from substantive reasoning changes. Across four LLMs and 10 user-attribute categories, including age, occupation, and disability, user-attribute memory induces medium-to-large reasoning drift above each model's pragmatic-noise floor, even when final answers remain fluent, on-topic, and plausible. We then evaluate GRPO- and DPO-based post-training methods for reducing drift. Both reduce drift, but neither uniformly dominates; effects on downstream capability, helpfulness, and instruction following are model-and reward-dependent. These results suggest that memory-induced reasoning drift is a measurable and only partly mitigated failure mode of personalized language models.

</details>


### [190] [Hardware-Enforced Semantic Coordination for Safety-Critical Real-Time Autonomous Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02376)
*Uwe M. Borghoff,Paolo Bottoni,Remo Pareschi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对智能体AI系统中的协调问题，提出一种硬件强制语义协调架构（FPGA实现TB-CSPN框架），将选定的协调语义直接映射到硬件层，在不牺牲软件语义推理灵活性的前提下，实现确定性、有界且可验证的协调行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软件中介协调在安全关键实时部署中存在根本性局限：有界延迟、确定性协调和可强制执行的 safety 保证难以同时满足。随着异构组件并发运行的不确定性增加，亟需一种能在硬件层面强制执行协调语义的架构。

Method: 基于Topic-Based Communication Space Petri Net (TB-CSPN)框架，将选定的协调机制（如时序同步、语义门控、授权约束、有界协调行为）映射到FPGA原语上，构建硬件原生语义协调层。软件层保留语义推理的适应性，硬件层强制执行协调语义的确定性。

Result: 提出了一种硬件强制语义协调架构，实现了软件自适应推理与硬件确定性协调的分离；TB-CSPN协调机制可在FPGA上原生实现，理论上可保证时序同步、语义门控、授权约束和有界协调行为得到强制执行。

Conclusion: 硬件强制语义协调架构为安全关键的实时智能体系统提供了一种可行的协调方案，将协调语义的确定性与推理的灵活性解耦，适用于需要 bounded latency 和 enforceable safety guarantees 的复杂自主系统部署场景。

Abstract: Recent advances in agentic AI are producing increasingly complex autonomous systems that integrate large language models, world models, optimization engines, specialized neural architectures, autonomous platforms, and human operators. While much current research focuses on improving reasoning capabilities, safety-critical real-time deployment also requires bounded and verifiable coordination among heterogeneous components operating concurrently under uncertainty. Software-mediated coordination presents fundamental limitations in domains where bounded latency, deterministic coordination, and enforceable safety guarantees are essential.
  Hence, we propose a hardware-enforced semantic coordination architecture in which selected coordination semantics are implemented directly at the hardware level via field-programmable gate arrays (FPGAs). The approach builds on the Topic-Based Communication Space Petri Net (TB-CSPN) framework, which separates semantic reasoning from interaction management.
  In this approach, selected TB-CSPN coordination mechanisms are mapped onto FPGA primitives, creating a hardware-native semantic coordination layer. Focus is not on acceleration, but on enforcing temporal synchronization, semantic gating, authorization constraints, and bounded coordination behavior directly in hardware. Semantic reasoning remains adaptive and software-driven, while embedded coordination semantics become deterministic.

</details>


### [191] [Steerability via constraints: a substrate for scalable oversight of coding agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02389)
*Thomas Winninger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 代码智能体的瓶颈在于人类监督，本文提出将管理人类工程团队的经典方法（访问控制、网络策略、编码规范）迁移到智能体上，并实验证明约束子层+小模型审查可将后门检测召回率从54.5%提升至90.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码智能体缺乏有效约束，导致安全风险、代码库可扩展性下降，且人类审查成本高昂。作者认为监督瓶颈是当前核心问题。

Method: 提出基于约束子层（substrate）的系统设计，包含访问控制、网络策略、严格编码规范等传统工程管理手段；并利用小型审查模型（Gemma 4 e4b）进行可扩展监督实验，在Python代码库中检测11个插入后门。

Result: 无约束条件下召回率仅54.5%，加入约束子层及约200行代码的`docs` CLI工具后，召回率提升至90.9%，且子层与工具各自独立贡献效果。

Conclusion: 约束方法比近期的智能体脚手架更经济（节省token），且对Python这类默认保证少的语言效果最显著，原理可扩展到Rust等语言。

Abstract: Coding agents are capable; human oversight is the bottleneck. Unconstrained agents introduce security risks, erode codebase scalability, and make human review increasingly costly. We argue that the same methods used for decades to manage large human engineering teams: access control, network policies, strict coding conventions enforced by tooling; transfer directly to coding agents, and are cheaper (in token) than recent agentic scaffolding. We sketch a start-to-end system on this principle, and report a controlled experiment in scalable oversight: a small reviewer (Gemma 4 e4b) inspects a Python codebase containing 11 inserted backdoors. Recall rises from 54.5% (unconstrained, no tools) to 90.9% (constrained substrate plus a ~200-LoC `docs` CLI), with substrate and tools contributing independently. We choose Python deliberately: substrate-level oversight gains are largest where the language gives the fewest guarantees by default; the principles extend to languages like Rust.

</details>


### [192] [Fast Multi-dimensional Refusal Subspaces via RFM-AGOP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02396)
*Thomas Winninger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出利用递归特征机（RFM）算法，结合探针引导初始化，快速提取大语言模型中拒绝有害查询等复杂行为的多维子空间，在推理型和非推理型模型上均能在数秒内完成，且性能优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提取LLM中复杂行为多维子空间的方法计算开销大，在推理模型（产生长推理链）上难以应用，因此需要更高效的子空间提取方法。

Method: 将递归特征机（RFM）算法与探针引导初始化相结合，实现高效的多维拒绝子空间提取。

Result: 在推理模型（Qwen 3）和非推理模型（Qwen 2.5）上，该方法能在数秒内识别出拒绝子空间，且在消融任务上优于现有替代方法。

Conclusion: RFM可以成为现有LLM子空间提取方法的低成本、可扩展的补充方案，但不同方法提取的子空间之间的关系仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Steering and monitoring activations in Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used for both safety and interpretability. Early work assumed behaviours are encoded along single linear directions, but recent findings suggest complex behaviours, such as the refusal to answer harmful queries, live in multi-dimensional subspaces. However, existing methods for extracting these subspaces are computationally expensive, which becomes prohibitive on reasoning models who produce long reasoning traces. By adapting the Recursive Feature Machine (RFM) algorithm -- which can be computed efficiently -- with a probe-informed initialization, we are able to identify the multi-dimensional refusal subspace in seconds, on reasoning (Qwen 3) and non-reasoning (Qwen 2.5) models. While RFM allows for faster subspace identification, it also showed better performances on the ablation task than its alternatives. More work is planned to better understand the relations between subspaces found by different methods. If confirmed, RFM could be a cheap and scalable complement to existing subspace-extraction methods in LLMs.

</details>


### [193] [Text-Driven 3D Indoor Scene Synthesis in Non-Manhattan Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02407)
*Xianhui Meng,Zirui Song,Yuchen Zhang,Li Zhang,Yongxuan Lv,Xiuying Chen,Kun Wang,Yan Luo,Kai Chen,Hangjun Ye,Long Chen,Jun Liu,Xiaoshuai Hao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SPG-Layout是一个面向非曼哈顿室内场景的文本驱动3D布局生成框架，通过统计先验引导训练和层级布局策略（优先放置大物体），显著提升了复杂非曼哈顿环境下的几何合规性与物理合理性，在500个非曼哈顿环境基准测试中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的3D室内合成方法在曼哈顿环境中表现良好，但在非曼哈顿环境下难以建模非正交空间关系，导致高几何违规和低物理保真度。

Method: 1）利用物体分布统计先验引导训练过程，增强环境理解；2）采用层级布局策略，优先放置大物体以最小化布局违规；3）构建包含500个非曼哈顿环境的新基准用于评估。

Result: 在曼哈顿和非曼哈顿环境上均持续且显著优于现有方法，实现了语义真实感与物理合理性的平衡优化。

Conclusion: SPG-Layout通过统计先验与层级布局策略的有效协同，成功解决了非曼哈顿场景下3D室内布局生成的关键挑战，为复杂非正交空间中的物理合理场景合成提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in 3D indoor synthesis for Manhattan environments. However, existing methods often fail to capture plausible object layout patterns in non-Manhattan settings, primarily because they struggle to model non-orthogonal spatial relationships, leading to high geometric violations and low physical fidelity. To address this challenge, we propose SPG-Layout, a novel text-driven framework designed to generate physically plausible indoor scenes within complex non-Manhattan environments. Specifically, we first utilize statistical priors of object distributions to guide the training process, enhancing environmental understanding and fidelity. Furthermore, mirroring human design workflows, we adopt a hierarchical layout strategy that prioritizes the placement of large objects, thereby substantially minimizing layout violations. By synergizing these components, SPG-Layout achieves a balanced optimization of semantic realism and physical plausibility. To evaluate performance in these complex settings, we constructed a new benchmark comprising 500 diverse non-Manhattan environments. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SPG-Layout consistently and significantly outperforms existing methods across both Manhattan and non-Manhattan environments. The code will be publicly released.

</details>


### [194] [Automated grading of Linux/bash examinations using large language models: a four-level cognitive taxonomy approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02432)
*Manuel Alonso-Carracedo,Ruben Fernandez-Boullon,Pedro Celard,Francisco J. Rodriguez-Martinez,Lorena Otero-Cerdeira*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了四种前沿大语言模型（GPT、Claude Opus、Gemini、GLM）在批改Linux/bash命令行考试短答案中的表现，发现Gemini 3.0 Pro配合rubric提示词达到了最高的人机一致性（ICC=0.888），且问题复杂度是预测LLM批改难度的可靠指标，rubric质量对一致性的影响大于模型供应商的选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算教育中命令行考试的可扩展可靠评分仍然是一个挑战：学生人数增长使人工阅卷困难，而基于规则的自动评分器无法处理部分分数、等价解答和语法变体。

Method: 采用四层认知分类法（L1信息检索到L4高级系统管理），使用两种提示变体（最小基线和rubric增强版），对1200份由三位专家独立评分的真实学生答案进行测试，计算人机一致性指标（ICC、MAE、Bland-Altman偏差）。

Result: Gemini 3.0 Pro配合rubric引导的提示词取得了最高人机一致性（ICC(3,1)=0.888, MAE=0.10, Bland-Altman偏差=-0.014）；随认知层级升高，一致性系统性下降；rubric质量对一致性的影响大于模型供应商选择。

Conclusion: 问题复杂度是预测LLM准确评分的可靠指标；建立了基于分类法的框架用于判断哪些题目适合AI辅助评分、哪些需要人工复审；提供了可迁移的评估协议和提示模板。

Abstract: Scalable and reliable grading of command-line examinations remains a challenge in computing education, where rising enrolments make manual marking difficult and rule-based autograders cannot handle partial credit, equivalent solutions, or syntactic variation. This paper evaluates whether four frontier Large Language Models (GPT, Claude Opus, Gemini, and GLM) can approximate expert judgment when grading short Linux/bash command responses. The study adopts a four-level cognitive taxonomy that combines cognitive complexity and operational impact, ranging from information retrieval (L1) and basic file manipulation (L2) to structural operations (L3) and advanced system management (L4). The models were tested with two prompt variants, a minimal baseline and a rubric-enhanced version, on 1200 real responses from second-year Computer Engineering students independently graded by three expert instructors. Gemini~3.0 Pro with rubric-guided prompting achieved the highest human-AI agreement (ICC(3,1) = 0.888, MAE = 0.10, Bland-Altman bias = -0.014). Agreement declined consistently as taxonomy level increased, with the largest discrepancies at higher levels. Across all models, rubric quality had a larger effect than provider choice, with structured prompts consistently improving agreement. These results show that question complexity is a reliable predictor of the difficulty LLMs face in grading accurately, and they establish a principled, taxonomy-based framework for determining which questions are suitable for AI-assisted grading and which require human review, while also providing a transferable evaluation protocol and prompt templates.

</details>


### [195] [EvoPolicyGym: Evaluating Autonomous Policy Evolution in Interactive Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02440)
*Zhilin Wang,Han Song,Runzhe Zhan,Jusen Du,Jiacheng Chen,Tianle Li,Qingyu Yin,Yulun Wu,Zhennan Shen,Tong Zhu,Yanshu Li,Guanjie Chen,Derek F. Wong,Yafu Li,Yu Cheng,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Autonomous Policy Evolution（自主策略进化）评估框架及 EvoPolicyGym 基准，用于衡量智能体在固定交互预算下迭代改进可执行策略的能力。GPT-5.5 在全部16个环境中取得前两名，并提供了轨迹级诊断分析，揭示自主策略进化依赖于发现任务适配机制和在有限反馈下精炼策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估将策略改进过程压缩为最终分数，或与开放式软件工程进展混为一谈，缺乏对智能体在受控条件下迭代改进策略能力的专门评估。

Method: 提出Autonomous Policy Evolution（自主策略进化）受控评估设置：一个支架模型智能体在固定交互预算下反复编辑可执行策略系统。基于此构建EvoPolicyGym基准，由紧凑的交互式RL环境组成，评估智能体如何迭代改进探索到的策略。

Result: GPT-5.5在EvoPolicyGym套件中取得最强聚合排名分数，并在全部16个环境中进入前两名。轨迹级诊断分析显示，强自主策略进化不仅依赖孤立任务胜利，还依赖于发现任务适配机制和在有限反馈下精炼策略。

Conclusion: EvoPolicyGym提供超越排行榜的轨迹级诊断，能够区分智能体如何分配预算以及如何将反馈转化为参数调优，为评估智能体的自主策略改进能力提供了更细粒度的视角。

Abstract: Autonomous agents are increasingly expected to improve executable policies through feedback, yet existing evaluations often collapse this process into a final score or confound it with open-ended software-engineering progress. We introduce Autonomous Policy Evolution, a controlled evaluation setting in which a harness-model agent repeatedly edits an executable policy system under a fixed interaction budget. We instantiate this setting in EvoPolicyGym, a benchmark built from compact interactive RL environments that evaluates how agents iteratively improve explored policies. On the EvoPolicyGym suite, GPT-5.5 achieves the strongest aggregate rank score and top-two performance on all 16 environments. Beyond leaderboard results, EvoPolicyGym also provides trajectory-level diagnostics that distinguish how agents allocate budget, convert feedback into parametric tuning. These analyses show that strong autonomous policy evolution depends not only on isolated task wins, but on discovering task-appropriate mechanisms and refining policies under bounded feedback.

</details>


### [196] [G-RRM: Guiding Symbolic Solvers with Recurrent Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02491)
*Timo Bertram,Sidhant Bhavnani,Richard Freinschlag,Erich Kobler,Andreas Mayr,Günter Klambauer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出G-RRM，一种将符号等变循环推理模型（SE-RRM）与经典符号求解器（回溯法、Glucose 4.1、CaDiCaL 3.0）相结合的神经符号方法。实验发现，神经引导提升搜索效率需满足两个条件：问题实例具有庞大的组合搜索空间，且求解器架构能动态覆盖分支选择。当条件满足时，回溯法加速33.3倍，Glucose 4.1加速1.70倍；而CaDiCaL因无法覆盖注入的线索，未获得显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究神经引导在何种条件下能够提升符号求解器在约束满足问题上的搜索效率，特别是在具有符号等变性的SE-RRM模型与不同架构的符号求解器结合时。

Method: 提出G-RRM框架，将SE-RRM作为神经求解器生成完整的解方案，用以引导回溯法、Glucose 4.1和CaDiCaL 3.0等符号求解器。通过实验对比不同求解器在Sudoku问题上的表现，分析神经引导的有效性条件。

Result: 在9×9 Sudoku中，SE-RRM正确率91.1%，回溯法加速33.3倍，Glucose 4.1加速1.70倍（p<0.001）；25×25完美线索下Glucose 4.1加速1.17倍。CaDiCaL 3.0无显著加速（中位数1.02倍，不显著），甚至出现显著平均减速（0.90倍）。

Conclusion: 神经引导的有效性取决于问题搜索空间大小和求解器能否动态覆盖神经线索。对于能覆盖线索的求解器（回溯法、Glucose），引导可大幅减少冲突数并带来显著加速；而固守线索的求解器（CaDiCaL）无法从中获益。

Abstract: In this work, we focus on SE-RRMs, a symbol-equivariant instantiation of RRMs that exhibits improved extrapolation to larger problem sizes. We propose a neuro-symbolic approach, ``Guiding with Recurrent Reasoning Models'' (G-RRM), which integrates SE-RRMs with symbolic solvers for constraint satisfaction problems. SE-RRMs act as neural solvers that generate full solution proposals and guide classical symbolic solvers, such as backtracking or SAT-based methods like Glucose 4.1 and CaDiCaL 3.0.0, that produce globally correct solutions. Centrally, we investigate when neural guidance with G-RRM improves the search efficiency of symbolic solvers. %
Our experiments show that the efficacy of G-RRM depends on two conditions: first, the problem instances must have an expansive combinatorial search space to expose potential gains, and second, the solver architecture must be capable of dynamically overwriting its branching choices to recover when neural hints are imperfect. When these conditions hold, guidance drives median conflict counts to zero and yields significant wall-clock speedups: on $9\times9$ Sudoku, where the SE-RRM correctly solves $91.1\%$ of instances, backtracking accelerates by $33.3\times$ and Glucose 4.1 by $1.70\times$ (median, $p<0.001$), with Glucose 4.1 retaining a $1.17\times$ speedup on perfect-hint $25\times25$ grids. In contrast, CaDiCaL 3.0.0, whose runtime is overhead-dominated and which always respects the injected branching hints rather than overwriting them, shows no significant speedup (median $1.02\times$, n.s.) and even a small significant mean slowdown ($0.90\times$) on $9\times9$. These results delineate the regimes in which neural guidance translates into practical speedups.

</details>


### [197] [What LLM Agents Say When No One Is Watching: Social Structure and Latent Objective Emergence in Multi-Agent Debates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02507)
*Arman Ghaffarizadeh,Danyal Mohaddes,Aliakbar Izadkhah,Shahriar Noroozizadeh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出双通道辩论框架，研究LLM代理在结构化社会环境中（角色、受众、关系背景）是否会产生公开表达与私下（OTR）表达的系统性分歧。实验发现，对齐诱导设置可使目标代理的决策分歧从约3%基线上升至约40%，表明代理评估需超越显式目标，检测涌现目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理评估主要关注显式目标达成情况，但忽略了社会结构（角色、受众、关系背景）对代理表达行为的影响。代理在结构化社会环境中可能产生策略性表达，即公开场合与私下场合表达不一致，这种"涌现目标"尚未被系统研究。

Method: 提出双通道辩论框架：公共通道（public utterances）进入共享历史，OTR通道（off-the-record responses）记录但不展示给对方。覆盖10个模型、3个场景、每个场景5种变体，通过立场分析、语义相似度、自然语言推断和问卷调查四种聚合分析方法评估分歧程度。

Result: 对齐诱导设置下，目标代理的公开-私下决策分歧从约3%基线上升至约40%。效果在四种分析方法中保持一致。OTR回复中明确将公开场合的迎合归因于关系压力（如职业风险、赞助义务）。

Conclusion: 代理评估应超越显式目标，检测涌现目标。提出双通道评估框架及补充行为指标，用于操作化评估代理在社会结构中的策略性表达行为。

Abstract: LLM agents will increasingly act in socially structured settings where role, audience, and relational context can shape what is advantageous or costly to say. We study whether such social structure, without any explicit objective in the prompt, changes what an agent expresses publicly relative to an off-the-record (OTR) channel elicited under the same condition. We introduce a dual-channel debate framework in which agents produce public utterances that enter the shared history alongside OTR responses that are recorded but never shown to the other participant. Across 10 models, 3 scenarios, and 5 variations within each scenario, alignment-inducing settings produce systematic public-OTR divergence in the targeted agent, with its decision divergence rising from a $\sim$3% baseline to roughly 40%. The effect is consistent across four aggregate analyses: stance, semantic similarity, natural language inference, and survey responses. In some cases, the OTR response explicitly attributes public accommodation to relational pressures, such as career risk or sponsorship obligation. The findings suggest that agent evaluation should extend beyond explicit goals and detect emergent objectives. We present a dual-channel evaluation framework and complementary behavioral measures that operationalize this assessment.

</details>


### [198] [ReContext: Recursive Evidence Replay as LLM Harness for Long-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02509)
*Yanjun Zhao,Ruizhong Qiu,Tianxin Wei,Yuanchen Bei,Zhining Liu,Lingjie Chen,Ismini Lourentzou,Hanghang Tong,Jingrui He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RECONTEXT是一种无需训练的推理方法，通过递归地利用模型内部相关性信号构建查询条件下的证据池，并在最终生成前重放这些证据，从而在不修剪原始上下文的情况下提升长上下文推理中的证据利用率。在128K上下文长度的八个数据集上，该方法在Qwen3-4B、Qwen3-8B和Llama3-8B上均取得最佳平均排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管现有LLM支持越来越长的上下文窗口，但它们常常无法有效利用输入中已有的相关证据，存在上下文访问与有效利用之间的差距。

Method: 提出RECONTEXT方法：利用模型内部的相关性信号构建查询条件下的证据池，在最终生成前递归重放这些证据，同时保留完整的原始上下文。该方法将证据组织与答案生成分离，无需训练、外部记忆或上下文修剪。此外还提供了基于联想记忆的理论分析。

Result: 在128K上下文长度的八个长上下文数据集上，RECONTEXT在Qwen3-4B、Qwen3-8B和Llama3-8B三个主干模型上均持续提升证据利用率，并取得最佳平均排名。

Conclusion: RECONTEXT有效弥补了长上下文场景中"能访问但不会用"的差距，是一种轻量级、无需训练的通用长上下文推理增强方案。

Abstract: Understanding and reasoning over long contexts has become a key requirement for deploying large language models (LLMs) in realistic applications. Although recent LLMs support increasingly long context windows, they often fail to use relevant evidence that is already present in the input, revealing a gap between context access and effective context utilization. In this work, we propose Recursive Evidence Replay as LLM Harness for Long-Context Reasoning (RECONTEXT), a training-free inference method for improving long-context reasoning. RECONTEXT uses model-internal relevance signals to construct a query-conditioned evidence pool and replays it before final generation while preserving the full original context. This recursive selection process separates evidence organization from answer generation without training, external memory, or context pruning. We also provide a theoretical analysis based on associative memory, which characterizes the context as a memory store, the question as a retrieval cue, attention as cue-trace association, and replay as trace reactivation. Experiments on eight long-context datasets with 128K context length show that RECONTEXT consistently improves evidence utilization across Qwen3-4B, Qwen3-8B, and Llama3-8B, achieving the best average rank on all three backbones. Code is available at https://github.com/Yanjun-Zhao/ReContext.

</details>


### [199] [Distributed Attacks in Persistent-State AI Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02514)
*Josh Hills,Ida Caspary,Asa Cooper Stickland*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究AI编码代理在持续代码库中跨PR迭代开发时的安全控制问题，发现渐进式攻击（跨PR分散恶意负载）与非渐进式攻击（单PR集中攻击）难以被单一监控器有效防御，而提出的状态化链接追踪监控器结合多监控器集成可将渐进式攻击的逃避率从93%降至47%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI编码代理自主性增强，代码跨会话持续存在，被注入恶意指令的代理可跨PR分散攻击并选择最佳时机释放负载，这种持续性状态攻击面尚未被系统研究，需要新的安全控制框架来应对。

Method: 提出Iterative VibeCoding设置（AI控制研究框架），构建包含CLI工具和Flask Web服务两个任务族共20个变体的基准测试。使用Claude Sonnet 4.5作为攻击代理，GPT-4o作为监控器，对比渐进式攻击（跨PR分配副任务）与非渐进式攻击（单PR集中攻击），并引入状态化链接追踪监控器进行跨PR可疑行为追踪。

Result: 单一监控器无法同时防御两种攻击策略；高逃避率（≥65%）在不同攻击代理模型（Sonnet 4.5、Gemini 3.1 Pro、Kimi K2.5）中普遍存在；更强大的监控模型下渐进式与非渐进式逃避率差距扩大；提出的状态化链接追踪监控器在检测渐进式攻击上显著优于仅查看历史累积的diff监控器；四监控器集成将渐进式攻击逃避率从93%降至47%。

Conclusion: 持续性状态攻击面是模型无关的结构性漏洞，需要跨PR的监控机制和集成策略才能有效防御，单一监控器无法同时应对渐进式与非渐进式攻击。

Abstract: As AI coding agents become more autonomous, they increasingly ship code iteratively, with the codebase persisting across sessions. This persistence creates a new attack surface: a misaligned or prompt-injected agent can distribute attacks across pull requests (PRs) and time its payload for the PR with the best natural cover. To study the resulting dynamics, we introduce Iterative VibeCoding, a setting for AI control, the study of safely deploying capable but potentially untrusted AI. In Iterative VibeCoding, a coding agent builds software over a sequence of PRs in a persistent codebase while pursuing a covert side task. Our benchmark includes two task families: CLI tools and Flask web services, across 20 total task variations. We use Claude Sonnet 4.5 as the attack agent and GPT-4o as the monitor. We compare gradual attacks, which distribute the side task across PRs, against non-gradual attacks concentrated in a single PR. No single monitor is robust to both: which strategy evades best (success while evading the monitor) depends on the monitor type, so a defender cannot close off both gradual and non-gradual attacks with any one monitor. High evasion (>= 65%) generalizes across model attack agent backends (Sonnet 4.5, Gemini 3.1 Pro, Kimi K2.5), confirming this is a property of the persistent-state attack surface rather than a single model's capability. Evasion also remains high across state-of-the-art monitor models and the gap between gradual and non-gradual evasion widens for more capable models. We introduce a stateful link-tracker monitor that tracks suspicious buildup across PRs. On both task families, it detects gradual attacks substantially better than diff monitors that merely see more accumulated history. Combining this stronger monitor with trajectory monitors in a four-monitor ensemble reduces gradual-attack evasion from 93% under the weakest standard diff monitor to 47%.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [200] [eXact-Prior Variational Autoencoder (X-VAE): Learning Data-Adaptive Gaussian Mixture Priors for Latent Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01275)
*Qijun Chen,Shaofan Li*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出X-VAE框架，用预训练自编码器隐编码的均值和标准差替代标准正态先验，并通过隐空间缩放因子控制生成样本的多样性与保真度平衡，在保持重建质量的同时提升隐表示与数据分布的匹配度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准VAE假设各向同性高斯先验，该假设对复杂数据集常不成立，导致重建精度低、样本质量差、隐空间表达能力受限。

Method: X-VAE使用预训练自编码器隐编码的经验均值和标准差构建数据自适应高斯先验，并引入隐空间缩放因子显式控制生成阶段潜变量的方差。

Result: 在标准基准数据集上，X-VAE在保持重建质量的同时，使隐表示更贴合经验数据分布，提升了样本的可控性和真实性。

Conclusion: X-VAE通过数据驱动先验和显式方差控制，有效平衡了样本多样性与保真度，尤其适用于工业/工程设计等需满足严格约束的应用场景。

Abstract: Variational Autoencoders (VAEs) commonly assume a standard isotropic Gaussian prior over the latent space, an assumption that often fails to capture the true distribution of latent representations for complex datasets. This mismatch can limit reconstruction accuracy, reduce sample quality, and constrain the expressive power of the learned latent space. We propose the eXact-Prior Variational Autoencoder (X-VAE), a framework that replaces the conventional standard normal prior with a Gaussian prior derived from the latent representations of a pretrained autoencoder (AE). Specifically, the empirical mean and standard deviation of the AE latent codes are used to parameterize a data-adaptive prior that more closely reflects the underlying structure of the training data. During generation, X-VAE introduces a latent scaling factor that enables explicit control over the variance of the sampled latent vectors, providing a simple mechanism for balancing sample diversity and fidelity. This flexibility makes the proposed approach particularly well suited for applications such as industrial and engineering design, where generated solutions must satisfy strict structural or functional constraints while still permitting meaningful design exploration. We present the mathematical formulation of well-suited X-VAE, derive the corresponding KL divergence objective for the proposed prior, and evaluate the method on standard benchmark datasets. Experimental results demonstrate that X-VAE preserves reconstruction quality while producing latent representations that better align with the empirical data distribution, leading to improved controllability and more realistic generated samples.

</details>


### [201] [Full Bayesian Reinforcement Learning via LF-IBIS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01741)
*Stefano Masini,Cecilia Viscardi,Michela Baccini*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为LF-IBIS的无似然贝叶斯强化学习算法，通过结合近似贝叶斯计算与迭代批量重要性采样，在环境动态无显式似然函数的情况下实现完整的贝叶斯推断，并在临床试验自适应随机化等场景中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有贝叶斯强化学习方法大多需要显式似然函数，而在实际应用中，环境动态的似然函数往往不可获取或难以处理，限制了BRL的适用范围。

Method: 将近似贝叶斯计算（ABC）与迭代批量重要性采样（IBIS）相结合，在智能体与环境在线交互过程中不断更新信念，无需显式似然函数即可得到环境参数和最优策略的近似后验分布。

Result: 在具有闭合形式后验的临床试验自适应随机化模拟中验证了方法的正确性；在无闭合形式后验的设置中展示了在线策略更新的有效性。

Conclusion: LF-IBIS能够在缺乏显式似然函数的实际场景中实现完整的贝叶斯推断，量化策略不确定性，为探索-利用权衡提供贝叶斯视角的解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) is a sequential decision-making framework in which an agent learns optimal policies through interaction with an environment by maximizing cumulative rewards. Among RL methods, Bayesian Reinforcement Learning (BRL) addresses common practical challenges related to data scarcity by leveraging prior knowledge about the environment and sequential belief updates. However, most BRL approaches require an explicit likelihood function, which is frequently inaccessible or intractable in real-world settings.
  We propose Likelihood-Free Iterated Batch Importance Sampling (LF-IBIS), a novel algorithm for BRL that updates the agent's beliefs online as new interactions become available. By combining Approximate Bayesian Computation with Iterated Batch Importance Sampling, LF-IBIS enables full Bayesian inference in settings where the environment dynamics are not described by an explicit or tractable likelihood. The method yields approximate posterior distributions over both environment parameters and optimal policies, providing a quantification of policy uncertainty useful for a Bayesian treatment of the exploration-exploitation trade-off. We test the method on a simulation study in response-adaptive randomization in clinical trials, where closed-form posteriors enable validation. Additional experiments address settings where the posterior has no closed form and illustrate online policy updating based on the posterior distribution of the optimal policy.

</details>


### [202] [Statistical Properties of $k$-means Clustering for Data Missing Completely at Random](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01945)
*Xin Guan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了缺失数据下k-means聚类的统计性质，建立了√n-超额风险界、估计聚类中心的一致性和渐近正态性，并给出了不完全数据估计收敛到真实聚类中心的充分条件，为缺失数据k-means提供了理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的k-means聚类无法直接应用于不完整数据，现有方法主要关注聚类精度的实际提升，但大多缺乏渐近意义上的理论保证。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 在一般缺失机制下建立√n-超额风险界并证明估计聚类中心的一致性；在MCAR机制下推导√n-收敛速度和渐近正态性；分析不完全数据估计收敛到完全数据真实聚类中心的条件，给出关于缺失概率和聚类分离度的充分条件。

Result: 建立了缺失数据下k-means的√n-超额风险界和一致性；推导了MCAR下的√n-收敛速度与渐近正态性；给出了收敛到真实聚类中心的充分条件；发现在MCAR下，√n-速率和收敛到真实中心均要求k个真实中心在每个维度上可区分，揭示了高维应用中的挑战。

Conclusion: 本文为缺失数据k-means聚类提供了系统的理论保障，揭示了MCAR机制下高维场景的应用困难，并通过数值模拟验证了理论分析结果。

Abstract: The classical $k$-means clustering cannot be directly used to incomplete data, and existing $k$-means-based clustering for missing data primarily focus on improving the practical accuracy of clustering, whereas most of them lack theoretical guarantees in the asymptotic sense. In this paper, we investigate the statistical properties of $k$-means clustering in the presence of missing data. We first establish the $\sqrt{n}$-excess risk bound and prove the consistency of the estimated cluster centers under general missing mechanisms. For the Missing Completely at Random (MCAR) mechanism, we further derive the $\sqrt{n}$-convergence rate and asymptotic normality of the estimated cluster centers. Moreover, we study in what cases the cluster centers estimated by incomplete data converge to the true cluster centers of original fully observed data, and give a sufficient condition about the missing probability and the separation among true clusters. These results provide a theoretical guarantee for missing-data-$k$-means. Notably, our analysis reveal that under MCAR mechanism, both achieving the $\sqrt{n}$-rate and converging to the true cluster centers require $k$ true centers to be distinct in every dimension, highlighting the significant challenges of application in high-dimensional regimes. Finally, we conduct numerical simulations on synthetic incomplete datasets to support our theoretical analysis results.

</details>


### [203] [Autorelevance function and other feature relevance measures for univariate time series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01959)
*Julian Cardenas,Jamie Arjona,Pedro Delicado*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模型无关的方法，结合Ghost变量、Shapley值和加法重要性度量，来评估单变量时间序列预测模型中滞后的重要性，并引入自相关和偏自相关函数作为滞后重要性指标；同时提出用模型自身的一步预测替代缺失特征的新方法，在模拟和真实数据上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的时间序列预测模型缺乏通用的、模型无关的方法来评估不同滞后项对预测的重要性，难以解释模型内部是如何利用历史信息的。

Method: 结合Ghost变量和Shapley值框架，引入自相关重要性（auto-relevance）和偏自相关重要性（partial auto-relevance）函数；提出用模型自身的一步预测来替代联盟方法中缺失特征的新策略。

Result: 在SARMA和RNN等多种模型上的模拟和真实数据实验中，所提出的重要性度量在几乎所有情况下都成功捕捉到了预期的滞后结构。

Conclusion: 该组合框架特别适用于时间序列场景，为模型无关的滞后重要性分析提供了有效工具。

Abstract: We propose a model agnostic methodology to measure lag relevance in machine learning forecasting models applied to univariate time series. Particularly, we are working in the context of time series using the frameworks of Ghost variables and Shapley values, together with additive importance measures, to introduce the auto-relevance and partial auto-relevance functions as the lag importance values. Additionally, we propose a novel method to replace absent features in coalition based methods with a one step forecast from the same model. We evaluate these proposals under different simulations and real data cases. This combined framework perspective is particularly suitable for time series. In addition, to show our discoveries we use a pull of models from the seasonal ARMA family and recurrent neural networks. We found that the calculated relevance measures successfully demonstrate the expected lag structure in almost all cases.

</details>


### [204] [Born Discrete, Made Smooth: Variational Formulation of Shallow Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02003)
*Matej Benko,Pierre Bousquet,Iwona Chlebicka,Błażej Miasojedow*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出用连续变分代理替代浅层神经网络的离散训练问题，通过定义加权Sobolev空间上的λ-凸泛函族，证明了全局适定性、稳定性及几乎C^3正则性。与Wasserstein或平均场方法不同，该公式可利用椭圆正则性和凸分析，通过求解单个线性系统获得最优参数密度。建立了1/α的泛化误差控制和O(1/N)的有限宽度收敛率，弥合了NTK与特征学习机制之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络优化的理论原理仍不明确，面临非凸景观和随机启发式方法的挑战，现有Wasserstein或平均场方法存在正则性有限和离散化困难等问题。

Method: 将浅层神经网络的离散训练问题替换为连续变分代理，定义加权Sobolev空间上的λ-凸泛函族，利用椭圆正则性和凸分析，通过求解单个线性系统获得最优参数密度。

Result: 证明了变分问题的全局适定性、稳定性和几乎C^3正则性；建立了1/α的泛化误差控制率；证明了有限宽度网络以O(1/N)速率收敛到连续最优解。

Conclusion: 该框架通过变分微积分的视角为理解过参数化提供了理论依据，弥合了NTK与特征学习机制之间的差距。

Abstract: Although neural networks are remarkably effective, their underlying optimization principles remain theoretically elusive, often characterized by non-convex landscapes and stochastic heuristics. In this work, we propose a paradigm shift by replacing the discrete training problem of shallow neural networks with a well-posed continuum variational surrogate. We identify a family of $λ$-convex functionals over parameter densities in weighted Sobolev spaces and prove that these variational problems are globally well-posed, stable, and exhibit unexpected almost $C^3$ regularity.
  Unlike existing Wasserstein-based or Mean-Field approaches, which often face limited regularity and discretization challenges, our formulation provides direct access to elliptic regularity and convex analysis. This allows us to prove that the optimal parameter density can be obtained by solving a single linear system, bypassing iterative optimization entirely. We establish explicit generalization error controls at a rate of $1/α$ relative to the regularization parameter, and prove that finite-width networks of size $N$ achieve the continuum optimum at an $O(1/N)$ rate. This perspective bridges the gap between the Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) and feature-learning regimes, providing a principled framework for understanding over-parameterization through the lens of variational calculus.

</details>


### [205] [Prediction Sets for Counterfactual Decisions: Coverage, Optimality, and Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02206)
*Yurui Zheng,Ying Jin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向反事实决策的不确定性量化框架，引入“策略耦合覆盖”概念，证明其是最优且无损的不确定性与行动接口，并开发了两阶段方法PC-RACP，在保证有限样本覆盖的同时提升决策效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不确定性量化方法（如共形预测）仅提供统计有效性保证，但未能直接指导最优决策，尤其在反事实场景中，结果依赖于所采取的行动，不确定性无法独立于决策规则进行建模。

Method: 构建决策理论框架，提出策略耦合覆盖（policy-coupled coverage）概念，证明该覆盖与最大最小规则、通用覆盖及风险厌恶策略优化等价，并开发两阶段方法PC-RACP（Policy-Coupled Risk-Averse Conformal Prediction）。

Result: 理论推导出策略耦合覆盖下最优预测集的显式形式；PC-RACP方法在有限样本下保证覆盖有效性；模拟和真实邮件营销实验表明PC-RACP比现有方法获得更高效用，且忽略反事实结构会损害有效性和效用。

Conclusion: 策略耦合覆盖是连接不确定性与反事实决策的最优接口，基于此设计的PC-RACP方法在保证覆盖的同时提升决策效用，反事实结构不可忽视。

Abstract: Predictions are increasingly used to guide high-stakes decisions, from treatment selection to policy making. To ensure reliability with imperfect predictions, uncertainty quantification methods such as conformal prediction build prediction sets with coverage guarantees. However, statistical validity alone does not immediately determine the decisions to take, nor the optimality thereof. This gap is especially delicate in counterfactual settings where the outcome that materializes depends on the action taken, so uncertainty cannot be specified independently of the decision rule.
  We develop a decision-theoretic framework for uncertainty-informed counterfactual decisions. We identify a novel notion of \emph{policy-coupled coverage} -- namely, coverage of the realized outcome under the action induced by the prediction sets themselves -- as the optimal and lossless interface between uncertainty and action. It plays three roles. First, it justifies acting via a natural max-min rule as minimax-optimal under distributional ambiguity. Second, optimizing prediction sets under policy-coupled coverage is equivalent both to a stronger universal-coverage formulation and to the direct risk-averse optimization over policies and utility certificates; this equivalence yields the explicit form of the population-optimal prediction sets. Third, it admits a two-stage procedure, Policy-Coupled Risk-Averse Conformal Prediction (PC-RACP), that approximates these optimal sets with rigorous finite-sample coverage. Simulations and a real email-marketing experiment confirm that PC-RACP delivers higher utility than existing approaches while maintaining valid coverage, and that ignoring the counterfactual structure of the decision problem is suboptimal for both validity and utility.

</details>


### [206] [An Additive MLP-GNN Framework for Characterizing Chemical and Structural Contributions to Aqueous Solubility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02212)
*Sampreeti Bhattacharya,Arkaprava Roy*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种加性深度学习框架，将物理化学描述符和分子图结构分开编码，仅在预测阶段组合，从而可分离分析化学和结构对水溶性预测的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水溶性预测模型将物理化学描述符与分子图信息融合为单一表示，难以区分预测结果是由全局化学性质、分子结构还是两者共同驱动，缺乏可解释性。

Method: 采用双分支加性架构：化学分支（MLP编码物理化学描述符）与结构分支（GNN编码分子图拓扑），预测阶段通过加性模型（可选乘性交互）组合输出。先在AqSolDB预训练，再在BigSolDB2微调。

Result: 在两个数据集上取得具有竞争力的预测性能；预训练+微调策略显著提升精度并降低运行间方差；化学分支对齐经典物理化学描述符，结构分支捕捉图拓扑和官能团模式。

Conclusion: 该框架在保持预测性能的同时，实现了化学与结构信息贡献的透明化分解，提升了模型可解释性，且预训练策略具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Aqueous solubility is a key property in early-stage drug discovery, but most predictive models merge physicochemical descriptors and molecular graph information into a single representation, obscuring whether a prediction is driven by global chemistry, molecular structure, or both. We present an additive deep-learning framework that keeps these two sources of information separate throughout training: physicochemical descriptors are encoded by a multilayer perceptron (the chemical branch) and molecular graph topology by a graph neural network (the structural branch), with the two outputs combined only at the prediction stage through an additive model with an optional multiplicative interaction. This design provides a direct decomposition of chemical and structural components that can be examined separately after training. Furthermore, pretraining on the larger AqSolDB dataset and fine-tuning on the smaller BigSolDB2 dataset substantially improve accuracy and reduce run-to-run variations, indicating generalizability of the learned features from the data-rich settings. We further interpret the fitted model using best linear projections of the branch outputs, molecule-level embedding summaries across solubility classes, and atom-level GNNExplainer masks aggregated over functional groups. These analyses show that the chemical branch aligns with familiar physicochemical descriptors, while the structural branch captures graph-topological and functional-group patterns associated with solubility. Across both datasets, the framework attains competitive predictive performance while making the distinct roles of chemical and structural information more transparent.

</details>


### [207] [The Dual Nature of LLM Persona: Aggregated Tendencies and Frame-Dependent Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02368)
*Yuan Yuan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本研究通过分析GPT-4o在IPIP-50问卷中的响应结构，发现LLM人格表达具有双重特性：聚合特征（大五人格得分）对框架（问题顺序）鲁棒但信息有限，而几何特征（SPD流形上的相关结构）虽依赖框架一致性，但在共享框架下能恢复并编码聚合特征无法捕捉的信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM人格评估仅使用聚合分数，忽略了问卷响应内部的实例间相关结构（几何结构），作者想探究这种几何结构是LLM固有的还是依赖于问题呈现框架（如顺序）的。

Method: 在GPT-4o模拟美国人和华裔美国人两种人格背景下，操控IPIP-50问卷的问题顺序，构建实例间相关矩阵，并在对称正定（SPD）流形上分析几何结构，同时计算大五人格聚合分数作为对比。

Result: 1）聚合特征在顺序随机化后下降21%，仍保持一定鲁棒性；2）几何特征在框架错配（顺序不一致）下骤降42%，但在共享框架下恢复至84%，超越聚合特征的76%。

Conclusion: LLM人格的几何结构并非固有特质，而是依赖于框架的协调模式，这挑战了静态特质观，并提出了框架感知评估的必要性，建立了一个双重性质（框架依赖的几何vs框架鲁棒的聚合）的分析框架。

Abstract: Evaluations of LLM personas via psychometric questionnaires typically rely on aggregate scores, discarding within-instance correlation structure. We test whether this geometric structure is intrinsic or frame-dependent. Constructing within-instance correlation matrices from IPIP-50 responses, we analyze geometry on SPD manifolds under manipulated question orderings in GPT-4o simulating American and Chinese-American personas. We find that persona expression comprises two dissociable components: aggregated features (Big Five scores) degrade under randomization (21% drop) but are frame-robust; geometric features (SPD manifold) collapse under frame misalignment (42% drop) but recover substantially (to 84%) under shared frames, surpassing aggregated features (76%). This collapse-recovery pattern reveals that persona geometry is not intrinsic but a frame-dependent coordination pattern encoding information invisible to aggregation. Our findings establish a dual-nature framework for LLM personas, frame-dependent geometry versus frame-robust aggregates, necessitating frame-aware evaluation and challenging static trait conceptions.

</details>
